First search[gingko light 20x20 pillow cover hand painted], answer format: &lt;answer>search[blanket with fleece throw]&lt;/answer>. Valid answer is search[&lt;keywords>] or click[&lt;clickable>].\", \"max_actions_per_traj\": 9}, \"Alfworld\": {\"env_type\": \"alfworld\", \"env_config\": {\"eval_dataset\": \"eval_in_distribution\"}, \"max_tokens\": 512, \"max_workers\": 1, \"env_instruction\": \"You are an expert agent in the ALFRED Embodied Environment.\\nComplete household tasks by navigating and interacting with objects.\\n\\nYou should first reason step-by-step about the current situation. This reasoning process MUST be enclosed within &lt;think> &lt;/think> tags.\\nOnce you've finished your reasoning, you should choose an admissible action for current step and present it within &lt;answer>...&lt;/answer> tags.\\n\", \"parallel_friendly\": false, \"max_actions_per_traj\": 50}, \"SearchQA\": {\"env_type\": \"search\", \"env_config\": {\"max_steps\": 10, \"max_search_results\": 5}, \"max_tokens\": 300, \"max_workers\": 32, \"env_instruction\": \"You are a search agent answering questions by searching for information.\\nUse search[your query] to find relevant documents, and finish[your answer] to submit your final answer.\\n\\nYou should first reason step-by-step about the current situation. This reasoning process MUST be enclosed within &lt;think> &lt;/think> tags.\\nThen provide your action within &lt;answer>...&lt;/answer> tags.\\n\\nExamples:\\n  &lt;think>I need to find information about Ben Platt's father.&lt;/think>&lt;answer>search[Ben Platt father parent]&lt;/answer>\\n  &lt;think>Based on the search results, Ben Platt's father is Henry Platt.&lt;/think>&lt;answer>finish[Henry Platt]&lt;/answer>\\n\", \"parallel_friendly\": true, \"max_actions_per_traj\": 10}, \"Countdown\": {\"env_type\": \"countdown\", \"env_config\": null, \"max_tokens\": 100, \"max_workers\": 32, \"env_instruction\": \"You are solving the Countdown puzzle. You should use the num list to create an equation that equals the target. Example answer format: &lt;think> To find an equation using [3, 5, 2] to get 4. Let's check 2 + 5 = 7, 7 - 3 = 4. So the answer is 2 + 5 - 3 = 4. &lt;/think>&lt;answer>2 + 5 - 3&lt;/answer>\", \"parallel_friendly\": false, \"max_actions_per_traj\": 1}, \"DeepCoder\": {\"env_type\": \"deepcoder\", \"env_config\": {\"max_steps\": 1}, \"max_tokens\": 8000, \"max_workers\": 32, \"env_instruction\": \"You are solving a coding task. Provide a complete Python function solution only. Format: &lt;answer>...&lt;/answer>\", \"parallel_friendly\": true, \"max_actions_per_traj\": 1}, \"game_2048\": {\"env_type\": \"game_2048\", \"env_config\": null, \"max_tokens\": 8192, \"env_instruction\": \"You are playing the 2048 game on a 4x4 grid. Merge equal tiles by sliding Up, Right, Down, or Left.\\nIf a move is invalid (no tiles move), a small penalty is applied. Respond with a single action.\\nExample: &lt;answer>Up&lt;/answer>\\n\", \"max_actions_per_traj\": 700}, \"AlfworldSG\": {\"env_type\": \"alfworld\", \"env_config\": {\"eval_dataset\": \"eval_in_distribution\"}, \"max_tokens\": 512, \"max_workers\": 1, \"env_instruction\": \"You are an expert agent in the ALFRED Embodied Environment.\\nComplete household tasks by navigating and interacting with objects.\\n\\nBefore each action, you MUST first produce a self-guidance assessment in your reasoning:\\n[Assessment: positive/neutral/negative] - evaluate your current progress toward the task goal.\\n[Reasoning: one sentence analyzing what has been accomplished and what remains.]\\n[Suggestion: the best next action from admissible actions.]\\n\\nYour reasoning MUST be enclosed within &lt;think> &lt;/think> tags.\\nAfter the closing &lt;/think> tag, output EXACTLY ONE admissible action command directly.\\nThe action must be a precise ALFWorld command from the admissible actions list (e.g. go to desk 1, pick up pen 2, open drawer 1).\\nDo NOT wrap the action in any tags. Do NOT output natural language descriptions or anything after the action.\\n\\nExample: &lt;think>[Assessment: neutral] I need to find a pen. The desk is a likely location. [Suggestion: go to desk 1]&lt;/think>go to desk 1\\n\", \"parallel_friendly\": false, \"max_actions_per_traj\": 50}, \"BanditTest\": {\"env_type\": \"bandit\", \"env_config\": {\"split\": \"test\"}, \"max_tokens\": 100, \"max_workers\": 32, \"env_instruction\": \"\", \"parallel_friendly\": false, \"max_actions_per_traj\": 1}, \"FrozenLake\": {\"env_type\": \"frozen_lake\", \"env_config\": {\"success_rate\": 0.8}, \"max_tokens\": 100, \"max_workers\": 32, \"env_instruction\": \"You are solving the FrozenLake puzzle. Valid answer is search[&lt;keywords>] or click[&lt;clickable>].\", \"max_actions_per_traj\": 9}, \"Alfworld\": {\"env_type\": \"alfworld\", \"env_config\": {\"eval_dataset\": \"eval_in_distribution\"}, \"max_tokens\": 512, \"max_workers\": 1, \"env_instruction\": \"You are an expert agent in the ALFRED Embodied Environment.\\nComplete household tasks by navigating and interacting with objects.\\n\\nYou should first reason step-by-step about the current situation. This reasoning process MUST be enclosed within &lt;think> &lt;/think> tags.\\nOnce you've finished your reasoning, you should choose an admissible action for current step and present it within &lt;answer>...&lt;/answer> tags.\\n\", \"parallel_friendly\": false, \"max_actions_per_traj\": 50}, \"SearchQA\": {\"env_type\": \"search\", \"env_config\": {\"max_steps\": 10, \"max_search_results\": 5}, \"max_tokens\": 300, \"max_workers\": 32, \"env_instruction\": \"You are a search agent answering questions by searching for information.\\nUse search[your query] to find relevant documents, and finish[your answer] to submit your final answer.\\n\\nYou should first reason step-by-step about the current situation. This reasoning process MUST be enclosed within &lt;think> &lt;/think> tags.\\nThen provide your action within &lt;answer>...&lt;/answer> tags.\\n\\nExamples:\\n  &lt;think>I need to find information about Ben Platt's father.&lt;/think>&lt;answer>search[Ben Platt father parent]&lt;/answer>\\n  &lt;think>Based on the search results, Ben Platt's father is Henry Platt.&lt;/think>&lt;answer>finish[Henry Platt]&lt;/answer>\\n\", \"parallel_friendly\": true, \"max_actions_per_traj\": 10}, \"Countdown\": {\"env_type\": \"countdown\", \"env_config\": null, \"max_tokens\": 100, \"max_workers\": 32, \"env_instruction\": \"You are solving the Countdown puzzle. So the answer is 2 + 5 - 3 = 4. &lt;/think>&lt;answer>2 + 5 - 3&lt;/answer>\", \"parallel_friendly\": false, \"max_actions_per_traj\": 1}, \"DeepCoder\": {\"env_type\": \"deepcoder\", \"env_config\": {\"max_steps\": 1}, \"max_tokens\": 8000, \"max_workers\": 32, \"env_instruction\": \"You are solving a coding task. Provide a complete Python function solution only. Format: &lt;answer>...&lt;/answer>\", \"parallel_friendly\": true, \"max_actions_per_traj\": 1}, \"game_2048\": {\"env_type\": \"game_2048\", \"env_config\": null, \"max_tokens\": 8192, \"env_instruction\": \"You are playing the 2048 game on a 4x4 grid. Merge equal tiles by sliding Up, Right, Down, or Left.\\nIf a move is invalid (no tiles move), a small penalty is applied. Respond with a single action.\\nExample: &lt;answer>Up&lt;/answer>\\n\", \"max_actions_per_traj\": 700}, \"AlfworldSG\": {\"env_type\": \"alfworld\", \"env_config\": {\"eval_dataset\": \"eval_in_distribution\"}, \"max_tokens\": 512, \"max_workers\": 1, \"env_instruction\": \"You are an expert agent in the ALFRED Embodied Environment.\\nComplete household tasks by navigating and interacting with objects.\\n\\nBefore each action, you MUST first produce a self-guidance assessment in your reasoning:\\n[Assessment: positive/neutral/negative] - evaluate your current progress toward the task goal.\\n[Reasoning: one sentence analyzing what has been accomplished and what remains.]\\n[Suggestion: the best next action from admissible actions.]\\n\\nYour reasoning MUST be enclosed within &lt;think> &lt;/think> tags.\\nAfter the closing &lt;/think> tag, output EXACTLY ONE admissible action command directly.\\nThe action must be a precise ALFWorld command from the admissible actions list (e.g. go to desk 1, pick up pen 2, open drawer 1).\\nDo NOT wrap the action in any tags. Do NOT output natural language descriptions or anything after the action.\\n\\nExample: &lt;think>[Assessment: neutral] I need to find a pen. The desk is a likely location. [Suggestion: go to desk 1]&lt;/think>go to desk 1\\n\", \"parallel_friendly\": false, \"max_actions_per_traj\": 50}, \"BanditTest\": {\"env_type\": \"bandit\", \"env_config\": {\"split\": \"test\"}, \"max_tokens\": 100, \"max_workers\": 32, \"env_instruction\": \"\", \"parallel_friendly\": false, \"max_actions_per_traj\": 1}, \"FrozenLake\": {\"env_type\": \"frozen_lake\", \"env_config\": {\"success_rate\": 0.8}, \"max_tokens\": 100, \"max_workers\": 32, \"env_instruction\": \"You are solving the FrozenLake puzzle. Forbid the whole and go to the target. You may move to the unintended direction due to the slippery ice. Example answer format: &lt;think>To forbid the hole and go to the target, I should go left then go up.&lt;/think>&lt;answer>Left || Up&lt;/answer>\", \"parallel_friendly\": false, \"max_actions_per_traj\": 10}, \"MetamathQA\": {\"env_type\": \"metamathqa\", \"env_config\": null, \"max_tokens\": 100, \"env_instruction\": \"You are solving Math problems. \", \"max_actions_per_traj\": 1}, \"rubikscube\": {\"env_type\": \"rubikscube\", \"env_config\": {\"max_steps\": 20, \"render_mode\": \"text\", \"scramble_depth\": 1}, \"max_tokens\": 96, \"env_instruction\": \"You are solving a 2x2 Rubik's Cube (Pocket Cube). The goal is to restore the cube so that each of the faces consists of a single, unique color.\\nAvailable actions use standard Singmaster notation for face rotations: U, U', D, D', L, L', R, R', F, F', B, B'.\\n- Faces: U (Up), D (Down), L (Left), R (Right), F (Front), B (Back).\\n- Modifiers: A letter alone means 90° clockwise (e.g., 'R'). A letter with prime (') means 90° counter-clockwise (e.g., \\\"R'\\\").\\nRespond with a sequence of actions separated by \\\"||\\\".\\nExample: &lt;answer>U&lt;/answer>\\n\", \"max_actions_per_traj\": 20}, \"AlfworldOOD\": {\"env_type\": \"alfworld\", \"env_config\": {\"eval_dataset\": \"eval_out_of_distribution\"}, \"max_tokens\": 512, \"max_workers\": 1, \"env_instruction\": \"You are an expert agent in the ALFRED Embodied Environment.\\nComplete household tasks by navigating and interacting with objects.\\n\\nYou should first reason step-by-step about the current situation. This reasoning process MUST be enclosed within &lt;think> &lt;/think> tags.\\nOnce you've finished your reasoning, you should choose an admissible action for current step and present it within &lt;answer>...&lt;/answer> tags.\\n\", \"parallel_friendly\": false, \"max_actions_per_traj\": 50}, \"WebShopFull\": {\"env_type\": \"webshop\", \"env_config\": {\"dataset\": \"full\"}, \"max_tokens\": 200, \"env_instruction\": \"You are an expert autonomous agent operating in the WebShop e‑commerce environment.\", \"max_actions_per_traj\": 15}, \"CoordSokoban\": {\"env_type\": \"sokoban\", \"env_config\": {\"dim_x\": 6, \"dim_y\": 6, \"max_steps\": 100, \"num_boxes\": 1, \"observation_format\": \"grid_coord\"}, \"max_tokens\": 120, \"max_workers\": 32, \"env_instruction\": \"You are solving the Sokoban puzzle. You are the player and you need to push all boxes to targets.\\nYou are provided with a symbol grid and the zero-indexed coordinates of the player, each box, and each target. \\nCoordinates range from the top-left corner (0, 0) to the bottom-right corner (5, 5). \\nWhen you are exactly next to a box, you can push it by moving in the same direction. \\nYou cannot push a box through a wall, and you cannot pull a box.\\nThe answer should be a sequence of actions, like &lt;answer>Right || Right || Up&lt;/answer>.\\n\", \"parallel_friendly\": false, \"max_actions_per_traj\": 10}, \"MediumSudoku\": {\"env_type\": \"sudoku\", \"env_config\": {\"grid_size\": 9, \"max_steps\": 81, \"difficulty\": \"medium\", \"render_format\": \"with_feedback\", \"show_conflicts\": true, \"show_valid_numbers\": true}, \"max_tokens\": 150, \"max_workers\": 32, \"env_instruction\": \"You are solving a Sudoku puzzle. Fill in the grid so that every row, column, and 3x3 box contains the numbers 1-9 without repetition.\\nInitial cells are shown in [brackets] and cannot be modified. Empty cells are shown as dots (.).\\nPlace numbers one at a time using the format: &lt;answer>place 5 at row 2 col 3&lt;/answer> or &lt;answer>2,3,5&lt;/answer>\\nThe environment will provide feedback on valid/invalid moves and show conflicts if any occur.\\n\", \"parallel_friendly\": false, \"max_actions_per_traj\": 30}, \"SearchQAMock\": {\"env_type\": \"search\", \"env_config\": {\"max_steps\": 10, \"mock_mode\": true, \"max_search_results\": 5}, \"max_tokens\": 300, \"max_workers\": 32, \"env_instruction\": \"You are a search agent answering questions by searching for information.\\nUse search[your query] to find relevant documents, and finish[your answer] to submit your final answer.\\n\\nYou should first reason step-by-step about the current situation. This reasoning process MUST be enclosed within &lt;think> &lt;/think> tags.\\nThen provide your action within &lt;answer>...&lt;/answer> tags.\\n\\nExamples:\\n  &lt;think>I need to find information about Ben Platt's father.&lt;/think>&lt;answer>search[Ben Platt father parent]&lt;/answer>\\n  &lt;think>Based on the search results, Ben Platt's father is Henry Platt.&lt;/think>&lt;answer>finish[Henry Platt]&lt;/answer>\\n\", \"parallel_friendly\": true, \"max_actions_per_traj\": 10}, \"SimpleSudoku\": {\"env_type\": \"sudoku\", \"env_config\": {\"grid_size\": 9, \"max_steps\": 81, \"difficulty\": \"easy\", \"render_format\": \"with_feedback\", \"show_conflicts\": true, \"show_valid_numbers\": true}, \"max_tokens\": 150, \"max_workers\": 32, \"env_instruction\": \"You are solving a Sudoku puzzle. Fill in the grid so that every row, column, and 3x3 box contains the numbers 1-9 without repetition.\\nInitial cells are shown in [brackets] and cannot be modified. Since `_step=1`, that means the initial validation step has completed and we're partway through the first training step.

The actor metrics showing up in the summary but not in history suggests they're being recorded at a sub-step granularity—probably per PPO epoch within the current step—and the full step-level aggregation hasn't finished yet. The gradient norm of 19.44 looks reasonable, entropy at 0.275 isn't collapsed, and the KL loss of 0.0576 translates to an effective penalty of just 0.000288 after applying the coefficient, which is negligible.

I need to be careful about what actor/kl_loss actually represents—it's likely the raw KL divergence before the coefficient is applied, not a transformed value, so comparing it directly to the alert threshold of 0.01 should be valid. I need to clarify whether `actor/kl_loss` is the actual KL divergence or a transformed version specific to verl's low variance KL implementation, since that affects whether it crosses the 0.01 threshold. Given this is only step 1, I can't establish whether this value represents a genuine spike or just normal initialization behavior. Let me finalize the report with these caveats in mind.
